# L4D2 port evidence ledger

Single record for function/subsystem evidence, original scenarios, capability results and feature parity. Identities refer to `L4D2_BASELINE.md`. Confidence: **observed** (seen in an original run or the pinned binary), **supported inference**, **unresolved**.

## Original scenarios

### S1 — rifle vs. one common infected, c1m1_hotel (captured 2026-10-20)

Conditions: staged original, `map c1m1_hotel`, mode `coop`, `z_difficulty Normal`, `host_timescale 1.0`, 30 Hz ticks (tick spacing 0.0333 s), listen host, `sv_cheats 1`, `director_stop`, survivor bots kicked (`kick Rochelle/Coach/Ellis`), `nb_delete_all infected`, `give rifle` (weapon_rifle, clip 50, reserve 360, ammo type 3). Driver: `E:/l4d2_work/scene3.txt`; state script `E:/l4d2_work/capture/m0_capture.nut` (per-tick `AddThinkToEnt` think plus game-event callbacks, removed from the staged tree after capture); raw log/screenshots `E:/l4d2_work/scene3/`.

Inputs: `+forward` ≈1 s; `z_spawn common`, moved 60 u ahead along the nearest clear direction and aimed at; shove (`+attack2` ≈0.1 s); wait for the first hit; aim, hold `+attack` until the automatic reload starts; then a short burst, `+reload` with 40 rounds in the clip.

Observed transitions (tick: value):

| Behaviour | Observation |
|---|---|
| Ground acceleration | From rest with `+forward` at yaw 154.995°, horizontal speed rises over 11 ticks (36.665, 63.335, … velocity vectors (−33.230, 15.498), (−57.396, 26.777), …) to exactly 220.000 u/s (terminal (−199.376, 93.003)); wall contact at x = 416.031 zeroes the x component, the y component continues sliding (96.1, 98.8, 52.9) until stop. Compare magnitudes, not single axes. |
| Shove → primary delay | Shove at tick 146 (`t=51.2000`) sets `m_flNextPrimaryAttack` from 44.6667 to 51.9667 without consuming ammo. M3 recovery: the producer adds 0.7 s to the command time; the apparent 0.7667 compares a different clock (`m3_contracts.md`). |
| Common infected wake-up | `infected` spawned through `SpawnEntityFromTable`, and `z_spawn common` wanderers, stay idle when the player stands still nearby or fires five rounds into the air; a shove at 60 u, or proximity to a survivor bot, makes it attack. **Unresolved:** exact perception rule. |
| Infected melee | Normal difficulty: `player_hurt dmg_health=2`, health 100→98→96→94, hits 0.7 s apart (ticks 298, 319, 340), `type=128` (DMG_CLUB). |
| Rifle cycle | `m_flNextPrimaryAttack` advances by exactly 0.0875 from its previous value per shot (58.4208, 58.5083, …), not from `curtime`, so shots alternate 2/3 ticks apart. |
| Bullet damage | `infected_hurt amount=32` per body hit (hitgroup 3), two hits kill a 50-HP common (`infected_death headshot=0 weapon_id=5`). An earlier run recorded a head hit (hitgroup 1) with `infected_death headshot=1` and remaining health −15. |
| Last round | Shot that empties the clip (tick 487) sets next attack to previous + 0.1313 (62.6209 → 62.7522), not + 0.0875. |
| Auto reload | Clip 0 with `+attack` held: `m_bInReload` becomes 1 at tick 492 (`t=62.7333`, before the 62.7522 next-attack time), next attack = 65.0000; clip becomes 50 and reserve 360→310 at tick 558 (`t=64.9333`), one tick before 65.0000. `weapon_reload manual=0`. |
| Manual reload with partial clip | `+reload` at tick 671 with clip 40/reserve 310: in the same tick clip shows 0 and reserve 350 (the clip is returned to reserve), next attack 70.9667; at tick 737 clip 50, reserve 300. `weapon_reload manual=1`. |
| Event shape | One `weapon_fire count=1` per round; two `bullet_impact` events per round when the first surface is penetrated. |

Observed quirks to preserve: last-round 0.1313 s delay; reserve temporarily includes the returned clip during a manual reload; reload begins before the stored next-attack time; damage event reports `32` for the rifle.

### M3 fifth-pass runtime and unbuilt corrections (2026-10-21)

`E:/l4d2_work/m3/ded_20261021_integrated29_numeric/` exercised numeric `M3Begin`, 50/360 rifle state, native observer readiness and a naturally spawned common, then hit a client command-pump timeout. The subsequent `...integrated29_numeric_ready/` used a bounded actual `WM_NULL` responsiveness fence before one command submission—not an input retry or gameplay delay—and reached the baseline encounter, manual reload, both-view captures and competition shooting.

Competition then exposed a real dedicated-server modal: `UserMessageBegin: Unregistered message 'HapSetDrag'` (`events.log:387–388`, `dialog_ded.png`). The sender is the SDK-only non-X360 `CBasePlayer::Event_Killed` haptics path; the pinned original's 65 registered messages contain no haptics messages, and the target client already excludes their hooks for L4D2. The integrator has excluded that SDK branch for L4D2 instead of inventing protocol messages or suppressing the error. This source change and the current armed/gesture/shove batch are **unbuilt/unverified**, not a runtime fix claim.

Latest gesture correction supersedes the earlier unbuilt interpretation: original server `S124530` and client `C148290` take **looping** as their third argument and force **autokill=true** downstream. Reload cases 4/5/6/38 therefore require autokill=true, looping=false; passing literal false to the SDK's different third-argument API was wrong. No published build used that intermediate change.

Cleanup for the fenced failure was completed: driver joined with exit1, its log closed, audio exited0 with no reported errors; the fresh 04:42:37 process query found no owned game/observer/audio/driver. Subsequent original-native attempts retained separate cold-cache startup, console-wrapper and player-role failures; none establishes combat parity. **M3 remains open**, including actual lag/loss, reconnect, switch/drop, penetration/history and presentation gates.

The fresh original run `E:/l4d2_work/m3/original_net_20261021_menu_ready/` reached two real survivor participants, userIDs2/6. Its `admitted_native_rate.json` records native `interval_per_tick=0.03333333507180214` (little-endian bits `8988083d`), tickcount1952 (`a0070000`) and matching gpGlobals pointer bookends. Native field offsets are decimal24/hex0x18 for tickcount and decimal28/hex0x1c for interval—not interval0x28. It records **55 validated native player snapshots per user**, covering the strict player observer; this is bounded preflight proof, not complete per-tick coverage or combat parity.

That run stopped before the common/combat fixture: `result.json` reports a timeout waiting for userID6's active rifle with clip50/reload0, exit1 and `parity=not_evaluated`; `cleanup_errors.json` is empty. The later diagnostic below narrows selection without repairing weapon state. Target runtime evidence still belongs to published build29. The newer animation/mode/noise/damage-support and atomic both-DLL scene-cache source changes have no new build/publication or target acceptance.

`E:/l4d2_work/m3/original_net_20261021_selection_demo/selection_diagnosis.txt` records a closed original diagnostic with demos and the 56-handle inventory observer started **before** the single give-rifle/give-pistol/slot1 sequence. Two real TEAM2 humans passed bounded native preflight, with 63 validated player snapshots each. Host: 705 complete decoded usercmd prefixes, including command2180/tick2210 selecting rifle entity235 (serial14535). Remote: 693 complete prefixes, all weaponselect0 around its actual local slot1 record. The native decoder independently matched every recorded outer command number; both demo files still have incomplete terminal framing and remain PARTIAL, not full-file/every-command successes.

In the separate authoritative server clock stream, the remote rifle402/serial12069 was genuinely owned and active at global tick2211; dual pistol226/serial7678 was active at2215 while the same rifle remained owned/stowed with clip50. Do not infer ordering between demo and server streams from equal-looking clock numbers. **No positive remote selection request is shown being server-rejected.** The exact client inventory/HUD/focus/deploy cause remains unobserved. No common/combat fixture, timing adjustment, selection retry, forced ammo or parity pass occurred.

This attempt's driver25164 joined exit1 and closed its log; clients23772/20056 exited0 without force, cleanup errors were empty, and the fresh08:29:59 census found no owned runtime processes. Mixed-endpoint audio reported no errors; it is not per-sound parity. Work returned to held source integration, not another launch.

The subsequent unbuilt gesture correction preserves the paired native active-slot-only reset: S123B70/C147B80 reset only active slots, and S3411A0 retires sequence/activity to−1 without rewriting starttime. Constructor metadata defaults and inactive layers remain unchanged; explicit initialization of newly allocated slots is separate. ### Terror NAV — actual C++ reader/writer proof (2026-10-21)

Evidence: `E:/l4d2_work/nav/terror_nav_cpp_runtime_handoff.txt` and `cpp_smoke_actual_proof_summary.json`. The instrumented production server in `cpp_20261021_instrumented_28d/` executed the real `TerrorNavMesh`/`TerrorNavArea` loading, native grid/ladders/PostLoad and visibility binding on the controlled `m2_contracts` world; this is separate from the earlier Python structural audit.

| Physical input class | Actual C++ loads passed |
|---|---:|
| Genuine SDK v16/sub0 fixture | 1/1 |
| Original v16/sub14 | 113/113 |
| Original v15/sub13 | 9/9 |

All inputs reached exact EOF; the native footer reported `restore_error=0`. Observed totals were 701,055,764 bytes, 506,560 areas and 4,586 ladders. Retail NAVs retained their expected out-of-date status against the controlled map; no campaign BSP size/identity was rewritten. Per-file cost-bit, serialized-mask, native-blocking, geometry/provenance and bound-reference checks reported zero errors.

Nine real legacy inputs also completed production Load → Save → sub14 reload, with a separate `restore_error=0`. This diagnosed, rather than repaired, loss of 17,816 legacy local visibility entries: Save accepted the dirty state; reload had zero local entries and reported ready because the dirty latch is not serialized. All 489 inherited owner/target mappings survived exactly. No visibility rebuild, topology repair or unevidenced dirty-Save refusal was introduced.

All 123 input identities remained unchanged. Temporary proof hooks were removed, and all ten permanent NAV sources matched their retained hook-free pins. Final incremental L4D2 client/server and affected HL2MP client/server builds exited 0; this step did not rebuild shaders. Published server SHA-256: `0364034f1bda4ad02a31c99fdb7c127e4e5e493b430a527d9c2b134e2edd282d`.

The separate hook-free `cpp_20261021_production_29/` run loaded the native fixture, saved a 5,808-byte sub14 NAV into its disposable map root and reloaded it. Its native output confirms the save and rejects `l4d2_nav_cpp_corpus` as unknown. All three owned dedicated attempts exited normally with code 0; the final recorded process query found no game/NAV-driver processes. Retained failures were a temporary-probe compile error and unquoted colon-containing RCON paths returning at the command guard, not NAV parse failures.

**Not M5 or campaign parity:** real rescue-policy and flow-endpoint producers, ladder/elevator world bindings, legacy visibility generation, campaign BSP21/world traversal and live actor behavior remain open. The corpus contains no nonzero encounter or population-directory records. The separate seventh `ignoreNavBlockers` argument is queued for the next source window, not included in this frozen proof.

### Scene image — actual original/x64 API comparison (2026-10-21)

New production `shared/l4d2/terror_scene_file_cache.{h,cpp}` implements the private VSIF v3 reader without changing the stock 12-byte cache ABI. The original x86 console oracle is `E:/l4d2_work/m4/scene_original_probe/run_20261021_003/`; the actual x64 utility builds and executes this production source against native `VFileSystem022` in `scene_native_smoke_20261021a/run_20261021_002/`.

The corrected build and run exited0. **All 207 shared observation rows match bit-for-bit**: 30 scene queries, 38 sound/string rows, 109 short-buffer rows and 30 reload rows. The authentic cases cover 14 compressed hits, five raw hits and 11 misses, including a highest-selected-image miss despite a lower mounted image containing the scene. Guards remained intact. Sixty additional target invalid-sound-index observations return -1/NULL; those are not paired original observations in this oracle. All five original image hashes remain unchanged.

The retained pre-fix run001 differs in four last-speech fields. Raw original instructions prove that x87 multiplies by the **float32** constant `0.001f` widened for the product, then stores float32; the double literal `0.001` was wrong. The corrected `/fp:fast` native executable matches without tolerances. `run002/comparison.json` pins source snapshots, executable/PDB, input, status and output identities; `E:/l4d2_work/m4/scene_cache_integration.txt` contains the complete caller/app-system/VPC cutover packet.

**Boundary:** this is real production-reader execution in an isolated utility, not a full 81,944-entry C++ corpus or either game DLL's startup/playback proof. Current L4D2 client/server source now excludes the prebuilt scene-cache app-system and initializes the private typed v3 owner. Mounted-v3 game startup, choreography/response/audio playback and shutdown remain runtime-unverified. Provision the original scene images before DLL initialization; withholding them is no longer required by the SDK v2 app-system failure. No engine hook or original-content modification was used.

### Build42 hotel intro trace and scene provisioning correction (2026-10-24)

Native evidence packet: `E:/l4d2_work/fixes/proactive/intro_freeze/`. `info_director` StartIntro/FinishIntro write Director+656 at original server `0x1025E370` / `0x10266560`; ReleaseSurvivorPositions separately calls SetSurvivorPosition(NULL), which clears native FL_FROZEN 0x20. The authentic update entity override schedules FinishIntro and ReleaseSurvivorPositions **16 seconds after relay_intro_start** (14 + 1 + 1), independently of scene completion or a client acknowledgement. Cameras begin their one-second return at +15 seconds. The intro scene fires Trigger1 at its own +2 seconds to request concept `introC1M1`; it does not finish the intro.

The build42 console contains **93 total admission polls, not 93 intro-true polls**: 15 pre-intro, then 78 intro-true through script time 21.8000011. Intro was false at 5.93333387 and true at 6.13333368, so the native scheduled finish falls approximately at 22 seconds, after the final retained poll. The retained run terminates at the separately owned fatal glow contract, with four `base_player_teleported` diagnostics immediately before it. No retained post-finish poll establishes an intro deadlock, and no synthetic timeout, forced FinishIntro, flag clearing, retry or acknowledgement was added.

A distinct actual fidelity defect is `scenes_withheld: true`: all five private loose layers omit scenes and the original VPKs have no scenes entries. The original update VSIF v3 contains the hotel scene (CRC32 `b6a1200c`, summary 2000 ms; compiled bvcd v4 Trigger1 at 2 seconds), but the port reports it missing. The external provisioning diff copies and SHA-256-pins all five original images into their existing private layer mounts before either DLL initializes its cache; no original bytes, precedence or reader miss behavior changes. Original h-run first retained unfrozen/intro-finished polls are at 26.9000015 / 26.9333344 / 27.0333347 seconds. These absolute clock values are not a replacement timer. Packet is authored, not installed or runtime-verified.

| Content-only mounting, original precedence | working | M1 root `E:/l4d2_work/m1/mod_hl2mp/gameinfo.txt`: per original layer (update, DLC3, DLC2, DLC1, base) one `game "<staged>/<layer>/pak01.vpk"` entry followed by a content-only loose layer `I:/l4d2_stage/loose/<layer>` (hard links of `maps materials models sound particles resource scripts media expressions gfx` only), mounted below the target's own content. The target filesystem mounts the v1 VPKs (`path` shows `(VPK)…\update\pak01.vpk`). Probe `m1_cl_resolve` (CRC32 of payload): `materials/glass/urban_glass_03.vmt` → update VPK, CRC `16bd415a` = update directory CRC (base has `e2ccf826`); `ads/burger_off.vmt` → base; `ads/ad01.vmt` → DLC3; `maps/c1m1_hotel.nav` → update loose (overrides base); `cfg/autoexec.cfg`, `bin/server.dll`, `addonlist.txt` → not visible.
